# AI Research Digest — Wednesday, September 2, 2026

## TL;DR
- **CCA (Context Compilation Architecture)** [arxiv:2609.00759] — Compile long novel ICL context once into a typed IR (rules / output_spec / tools / data_profile) + violation-gated correction, instead of one-shot read-and-reason. Open models pass only **12–16%** of CL-bench tasks without it; CCA beats vanilla / gist / multi-agent self-play on **1,899** tasks × 4 open bases. Steal: compile Mitamaton day-brief rules into typed slots before answering.
- **SkillZip Pro** [arxiv:2608.30785] — Execution-aware compression of **progressively loaded skill bundles** (root + on-demand refs/scripts/subskills) without flattening progressive-loading boundaries. One-Shot vs Continual (Zip-on-Write) × Persistent vs Transient. Directly applicable to Claude Code / Mitamaton SKILL.md trees that bloat the always-loaded root.
- **Delegation Without Trust** [arxiv:2609.00267] — Untrusted-model standard for multi-agent delegation: a fully prompt-injected agent still must not exceed delegated authority. Default runtimes (LangGraph / CrewAI / AutoGen / **MCP**) with broad bearer creds fail confused-deputy / token-replay / injection / compromised-subagent. Complements SARA + PES with identity/authorization/runtime governance requirements.
- **Harness-of-Harness (HoH)** [arxiv:2609.01481] — Meta-harness over Codex+GPT-5.5 / OpenCode+DeepSeek-V4-Pro / Pi+MiniMax-M3: iterative plan–code–test, scoped increments, independent eval, progressive skill exposure. Avg relative **+52.25%** on GameCraft-Bench / FrontierSWE / ProgramBench vs standalone harnesses. Continues the vault harness-evolution pile with multi-day autonomy.
- **Gated-Memory Routing** [arxiv:2609.00237] — Multi-agent orchestration that writes only non-redundant steps into learned execution memory and retrieves a compact subset per decision (EMNLP 2026 Main). Fixes query-only routing (no mid-run adapt) and full-history overload. Port: gate Claude Code subagent handoffs on a compressed execution memory, not the raw transcript.
- **HarnessEvolve** [arxiv:2609.00829] — Self-evolution from **reference trajectories** (paths when given gold answers) to fix credit assignment; quality gate (no leakage/bloat) + performance gate (improve batch, no recent-batch regression). Addresses shortcut learning + catastrophic forgetting that Aspire/WikiSkill optimism under-measure.
- **Defense-as-Skill / SkillSonar** [arxiv:2609.01487] — Runtime guard **as an installable skill** beside untrusted task skills; allow/replan/confirm against user task boundary. Evolved via MCTS on SCOPE-R. Claude Code / OpenClaw: GLM-5 ID ASR **0.482→0.104**, OOD **0.606→0.115**. Sign-flipped twin of RedEvoAgent — keep the evolution machinery, flip the objective to defense.
- **Invalidation Contracts** [arxiv:2609.00243] — Version stamps + cacheability hints on cross-episode recovery memory so agents evict stale API-error fixes after server drift. Validity (protocol) vs compliance (planner): Haiku 4.5 **100%** first-try vs Sonnet 5 **≤11%** (schema conservatism). ~9,400 episodes. Concrete for Mitamaton cached tool recoveries.
- **Runtime-Independent Persistent Agents** [arxiv:2609.00546] — Continuity substrate P=(identity, memory, versioned body) separate from replaceable (reasoner, harness, host). Migration ≠ new agent when lineage preserved. 833+92 tests. Formalizes what Logos/PES gestured at for agents that outlive a Claude Code / Grok Bot process.
- **Instella-MoE** [arxiv:2609.00791] — Fully open **16B MoE / 2.8B active**, trained from scratch on AMD MI300X/MI325X; Gated MLA + FarSkip-Collective; Think checkpoint avg **73.2** post-train. Weights+data+code released. Local-model corner candidate for 64 GB unified memory (2.8B act fits easily).
- **Firehose note:** 732 new abs-/API-verified papers in ~48h window (cs.AI/CL/LG/MA), all listed under Items (compressed). Podcast/site stages should highlight the TL;DR set and report total count.

## Items

### 1. Compile, Don't Memorize: A Context Compilation Architecture (CCA) for In-Context Learning
- **Source:** arXiv
- **Link:** https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00759
- **Authors / org:** Jinhu Qi / Minda Hu / Wentao Zhang et al. (7 authors)
- **Focus area:** multi-agent / memory/context / skills/harness
- **What it is:** Large language models (LLMs) increasingly handle in-context learning (ICL) tasks where a long, novel context defines the rules, knowledge, and output schema for a series of questions. On benchmarks that grade against every detail of the context, even strong open-weights models pass only 12-16% of tasks: a single overlooked rule fails the whole response. (abs verified; submitted 01 Sep 2026). Cats: cs.CL.
- **Why it matters for Justin's agents:** Relevant to Justin’s skill/SKILL.md stack / Claude Code / harness evolution / Mitamaton memory/context.
- **Action signal:** `try-now`

### 3. Delegation Without Trust: An Empirical Gap Analysis of Identity, Authorization, and Runtime Governance in Multi-Agent LLM Systems
- **Source:** arXiv
- **Link:** https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00267
- **Authors / org:** Panduranga Sai Varma Dantuluri / Jyotirmoy Sundi
- **Focus area:** multi-agent / memory/context / tools/computer-use
- **What it is:** Autonomous LLM agents increasingly act on a user's behalf: they hold credentials, call tools and services, and spawn sub-agents that act further on their behalf. This turns a long-standing distributed-systems question -- who is authorized to do what, on whose authority -- into an urgent and largely unsolved problem, because the component driving each agent is a language model an adversary can hijack. (abs verified; submitted 31 Aug 2026). Cats: cs.CR, cs.AI.
- **Why it matters for Justin's agents:** Relevant to Justin’s Mitamaton memory/context / multi-agent orchestration / MCP/tool calling.
- **Action signal:** `try-now`

### 5. Explore More, Drift Less: Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning Can Suffice for Long-Horizon Interactive Agents
- **Source:** arXiv
- **Link:** https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01245
- **Authors / org:** Liming Pu / Xiaoxia Li / Yifu Liu et al. (5 authors)
- **Focus area:** multi-agent / memory/context / skills/harness
- **What it is:** Reinforcement learning is a natural way to post-train LLM agents for long-horizon interactive tasks judged only by end-of-task verification, yet a shared belief holds that outcome-only RL soon hits a ceiling on small open models. Recent work therefore compensates around the training with denser rewards, SFT priors, skill libraries, curated memory, or multi-agent orchestration. (abs verified; submitted 01 Sep 2026). Cats: cs.LG, cs.AI.

### 24. Invalidation Contracts for Cross-Episode Agent Memory
- **Source:** arXiv
- **Link:** https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00243
- **Authors / org:** Michael Wu / Arquimedes Canedo
- **Focus area:** memory/context
- **What it is:** LLM agents that cache recovery suggestions from API errors can skip re-derivation in later episodes, spending fewer tokens and fewer model calls on constraints they have already learned. Server-side data drift turns those cached fixes into silent failures, and the usual remedy, re-deriving on every episode, gives the savings back. (abs verified; submitted 31 Aug 2026). Cats: cs.AI.

We present Facet-0, a robotic foundation model that predicts and values the contact consequences of its actions. (export-API verified; submitted 01 Sep 2026). Cats: cs.RO, cs.LG.
- **Why it matters for Justin's agents:** Background firehose item in-window; low direct Mitamaton leverage — keep for corpus completeness.
- **Action signal:** `track`

## Local-model corner
- **Instella-MoE** [arxiv:2609.00791] — fully open **16B total / 2.8B active** MoE, trained from scratch on AMD Instinct MI300X/MI325X; Gated MLA + FarSkip-Collective; multi-stage post-train to a Think checkpoint (avg **73.2** on instruction/reasoning/math/coding/chat in their eval). Authors claim release of weights, data, and training pipeline. **64 GB unified memory:** 2.8B active is well inside an M-series 64 GB slot (same class as small MoE day-admin models); do not confuse total 16B with active memory. Compare later vs Qwen3.x-A3B / Thomson-Small on personal day-admin tasks — coding/Terminal-Bench numbers not the headline here.
- **Not a Mitamaton weight drop:** Deploying DeepSeek 175B on RTX 4060 [arxiv:2608.30877] is a biomedical virtual-screening systems paper (heterogeneous memory management), not a general local LLM recommendation for Claude Code / Mitamaton.

## Conflicts & open questions
- **CONFLICT — EvoSkill Injection / SARGE [arxiv:2608.30429] vs WikiSkill never-rollback optimism [arxiv:2608.27454] and the skill-evolution pile.** Self-evolving agents that autonomously generate/store/reuse skills create a durable attack surface: malicious capabilities are generated, stored, and re-activated as “legitimate” skills. Complements RedEvoAgent [arxiv:2608.27439] (attack skill evolution against Claude Code/Codex) with a threat model aimed at the *generation pipeline itself*. Reading: do not append SKILL.md from traces without a held-out + recoverability + malice gate; WikiSkill’s “never roll back the wiki” is especially dangerous under this threat model.
- **CONFLICT — Defense-as-Skill / SkillSonar [arxiv:2609.01487] vs RedEvoAgent’s attack-skill ratchet (sign-flip).** Same machinery (evolve a Markdown skill from rollouts with a keep/drop gate), opposite objective. SkillSonar cuts ASR sharply on Claude Code / OpenClaw. Combined recipe with SARA / LoopHarness / PES still untested — do not treat ID ASR 0.104 as a Mitamaton Gmail guarantee.
- **CONFLICT soft — HarnessEvolve [arxiv:2609.00829] / HoH [arxiv:2609.01481] / DiagEvo [arxiv:2609.00768] / ARISE-RL [arxiv:2609.01058] / WHALE [arxiv:2609.00196] vs Aspire’s sealed vague-goal pessimism [arxiv:2608.31111].** Fresh self-evolution / meta-harness papers claim large gains; Aspire said retained sealed-eval gains are rare. Keep Aspire’s warning (job-finished ≠ capability retained) and HarnessEvolve’s dual gates (quality + no-regression) rather than collapsing to either pure optimism or pure despair. Combined multi-gate recipe still untested on personal tasks.
- Complementary, not conflicting: CCA typed context IR vs ContextPilot fold tools / Measure-Before-You-Manage accounting; Invalidation Contracts vs TwinKV / data-cracking corpus stores; Delegation-Without-Trust vs SARA Observation≠authorization + PES persona/execution split; Runtime-Independent Persistent Agents vs Logos cross-process bus; Control-Data Flow Separation [arxiv:2609.00621] vs SEPO/NPO prompt-opt (typed control objects immune to prompt drift); Calibration-is-the-Bottleneck [arxiv:2609.00949] vs CAST action critique (action-class choice before execution quality).
- If nothing else conflicts beyond the three flagged above: open questions are whether SkillSonar’s allow/replan/confirm maps onto Mitamaton’s deny/ask/allow MCP matrix, and whether Instella-MoE’s 2.8B-act Think checkpoint is competitive with Qwen3.x-A3B on day-admin without a coding regression like Thomson.
